# sive.rs: source 251

Reviewed 2026-10-24, Europe/Warsaw. Source: [original page](https://sive.rs/book). Tracking parameters are not part of source identity.

**Intake completed for recovered material.** 486 distinct catalog works linked; 0 extracted candidates remain unassessed. Raw source evidence and extraction records are retained under [the intake directory](../data/intake_shelves_261024/). Coverage limits, where present, are stated below.

The user requested reuse of existing assessments. Existing hard exclusions and vetoes are retained. Existing scored books receive only distinct-source REL recalculation; repeated titles, comments, episodes and editions within this source count once. No repeated mention is treated as independent evidence of quality. New judgments have low confidence unless stated otherwise.

The complete captured reading index contained486book entries; linked reader notes were used for new judgments.

<a id="shelf-70e42bb1acde"></a>
### Waste Books — Georg Christoph Lichtenberg

Status: **recommended**. Sources: 251.

Aphorisms about observation, language and intellectual independence offer reusable distinctions rather than a sustained model. The source values their unfinished character; this supports learning density but limits causal depth and cumulative structure. First notebook publication began in 1800, not the 2000 reissue. Evidence: https://eprints.bbk.ac.uk/id/eprint/47626/1/Part%20One_MBrooker_PhDThesis.pdf

Eligibility: nonfiction explanatory work, with no established hard exclusion or serious veto in the inspected source report and available bibliographic evidence. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-e67a602bc090"></a>
### The Invention of the Jewish People — Shlomo Sand

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

Counterfactual V1, moderate confidence. Inspected source excerpts offer useful distinctions between state, nation and inherited identity, but the central novelty and suppression narrative misrepresents prior historiography. Michael Berkowitz identifies the mishandling of Yerushalmi and omission of relevant scholarship: https://reviews.history.ac.uk/review/973/print/ . Retain the source reader's positive assessment of the general nation-building framework; this does not cure the historical inference problem. Original Hebrew 2008 verified by https://religiondispatches.org/2009/12/16/jewish-pastisraeli-future-review-invention-jewish-people . D=6.5 from excerpts and conflicting reviews, low confidence; not a full-text audit. Broad historical argument has no single endpoint. National history and politics deductions both apply.

Veto V1; confidence moderate. Central historiographic suppression and novelty claims depend on distorted treatment of existing scholarship; specialist review gives specific examples. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-09a50160520f"></a>
### When Things Fall Apart — Pema Chödrön

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

The captured source notes contain practical observations on avoidance and emotion, but frame them as a Buddhist path of awakening and enlightenment. Those useful observations remain in the counterfactual assessment; they do not remove the religious-practice gate. Publisher evidence: https://www.shambhala.com/when-things-fall-apart.html

Veto V5; confidence high. The inspected source notes present a Buddhist path to enlightenment and prescribe devotional meditation as the central response to suffering. This is religious teaching and practice, not historical or comparative analysis of Buddhism. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-bc4056079ff6"></a>
### Fossil Future — Alex Epstein

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

Counterfactual V2, moderate confidence. The captured source passages frame mainstream energy evaluation as fundamentally anti-human and indifferent to energy benefits; this is central to rejecting contrary expertise. Compare the actual treatment of development, energy access, costs and trade-offs in https://www.ipcc.ch/report/ar6/wg3/chapter/chapter-6/ . Disagreement over fossil-fuel policy itself is not the veto. Retain useful discussion of energy density, reliability, nuclear energy and information intermediaries. https://www.penguinrandomhouse.com/books/678856/fossil-future-by-alex-epstein/9780593420416/ verifies 2022 and the thesis. D=6 from source excerpts and reader report, low confidence, with repeated evaluative framing. T11 environmental debate; T12=0 because energy systems are central. Forward-looking thesis H13=0.

Veto V2; confidence moderate. Central dismissal of mainstream energy expertise rests on an anti-human motive attribution contradicted by its explicit development and energy-access analysis. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-f9534a9a0fa7"></a>
### Willpower — Roy Baumeister; John Tierney

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

The limited-resource account is central in the source description. Large preregistered studies found null or much smaller effects than the original broad account implied; a smaller effect does not validate a universal depleted resource. Useful practical advice remains, but the foundational certainty is not justified. https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC8186735/ https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12422598/

Veto V1; confidence moderate. The book's central finite-resource account of self-control is presented with substantially greater certainty and generality than later preregistered evidence supports. Null and small-effect replications are both considered; uncertainty about the exact residual effect does not restore the book's original broad causal account. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-039c482e2fe6"></a>
### The China Study — T. Colin Campbell and Thomas M. Campbell II

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

Counterfactual V1, moderate confidence: broad claims against animal foods outrun ecological associations and generalize casein experiments. The source excerpts present these claims as broadly established. https://www.lexjansen.com/wuss/2014/30_Final_Paper_PDF.pdf reanalyzes the ecological data and explains inferential limits; https://deniseminger.com/2010/08/06/final-china-study-response-html/ identifies specific confounding and extrapolation problems and reproduces Campbell's response that his case also rests on biological models and other studies. The veto does not assume that the entire argument rests on one survey, nor reject benefits of plant foods. D=6 retains explanatory material and research narrative, low confidence from excerpts and debate. https://search.worldcat.org/title/54905518 records first BenBella edition 2005; earlier catalog year 2004 is not used. Disease T11; research argument rather than technical clinical training; H13=0.

Veto V1; confidence moderate. Central dietary conclusions rely on overextended ecological and experimental inference; counterarguments and the author’s biological-model defense are recorded. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

Counterfactual V1: specialist review identifies multiple concrete factual errors and a central unsupported explanation of persistent failure through allegedly fixed national character. Retain the review's acknowledgment of strong reporting on aid dependence and political events; D=6.5 is not reduced merely because E fails. https://bookshop.iseas.edu.sg/component/get/12809 documents both sides, including problems in introduction, chapter 17 and epilogue. https://www.publishersweekly.com/9781586487874 is a favorable counter-review. Original 2011; central period ends approximately 2009. Both national history and politics deductions apply. V1 moderate confidence.

Veto V1; confidence moderate. The specialist Contemporary Southeast Asia review identifies concrete errors and central unsupported inference from an allegedly unchanging national culture to contemporary failure, while acknowledging valuable reporting in much of the book. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-9cbb6786e1a0"></a>
### Conspiracy of the Rich — Robert T. Kiyosaki

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

Counterfactual V1: inspected source excerpts misclassify ordinary equity and mutual-fund shares as financial derivatives, then extend that category to ideas and money creation as a purported explanatory mechanism. This conflation undermines the central financial-literacy argument; cash flow is also treated as insulation from valuation risk without adequate qualifications. Source reports pervasive generality, D=3. Some fee-awareness content is retained, not erased. https://openlibrary.org/books/OL24267632M/Conspiracy_of_the_Rich verifies 2009. US crisis and finance incur both deductions; framework H13=0. V1 moderate confidence; title alone is not evidence of conspiracy failure.

Veto V1; confidence moderate. The inspected source excerpts make ordinary equity shares and mutual funds into derivatives and use that category to explain creating money from ideas. The resulting central financial explanation conflates ownership, contractual derivative exposure and metaphor; the veto is not based on the title. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.

<a id="shelf-fac07e602c7f"></a>
### The Think Big Manifesto — Michael Port and Mina Samuels

Status: **vetoed**. Sources: 251.

Counterfactual V3: source reader reports that sustained encouragement replaces useful mechanisms throughout; inspected notes and publisher contents corroborate repeated injunctions to think bigger with little explanatory support. D=2 represents that low learning yield; U/M reflect weak transferable models and causal explanation. https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/book/10.1002/9781118257982 confirms 2009 and contents. No political-extremism or mobilization veto inferred from manifesto/revolution language. V3 confidence moderate because full text was not inspected.

Veto V3; confidence moderate. The source reader reports book-wide motivational repetition with little usable content; inspected notes and publisher contents support encouragement rather than developed mechanisms or heuristics. The manifesto label is not the reason. Overall and density confidence: **low**; no full-text density audit is claimed.
